Generated by `node tools/guides.mjs` from the Calamity, Infernal Eclipse of Ragnarok, Secrets of the Shadows, and official Terraria wikis.

| armor |  |  | Magic Hat |  | name=Magic Hat\|The Magic Hat is useful if a better mage armor option does not present itself sooner. When combined with a gem robe, the Magic Hat increases the player's maximum mana. This is generally an unsubstantial buff for the player, unless using particularly mana-intensive weapons (i.e. Demon Scythe or Thunder Zapper). Nevertheless, the Magic Hat's offensive increases are powerful, and if obtained early on can remain serviceable for much of (if not the rest of) pre-Hardmode. | v:2275 |
| armor |  |  | Gem robes |  | name=Gem Robes\|Gem robes have unique ways in combining the effects, if using gem staves with them equipped.
